# FerroGate CLI command reference

<!--
Generated by `crates/ferrogate-cli/src/reference.rs`; do NOT edit by hand.
Regenerate after changing the command surface:
    FERROGATE_REGENERATE_DOCS=1 cargo test -p ferrogate-cli reference
The `reference` sync test fails CI when this file drifts from the command tree.
-->

This is the full `ferrogate` command surface the shipped binary assembles: the
derived top-level commands plus the generic Control Plane API resource families
mounted under `ferrogate ctl <group> <verb>` (issues #361-#365), whose subtree
is derived from the `ferrogate-cli-core` registry metadata. Shell completions
(`ferrogate completions <shell>`) are generated from this same command tree.

## Examples

```sh
# Install shell completions (bash/zsh/fish/powershell/elvish supported):
ferrogate completions bash > /etc/bash_completion.d/ferrogate

# Point the client at a Control Plane API endpoint, then use it by default:
ferrogate context create prod --endpoint https://control.example.com --token-env FERROGATE_TOKEN --use

# List a resource family through the generic `ctl` tree:
ferrogate ctl tenants list --output json

# Read system status through the shared typed client:
ferrogate ops status
```


## `ferrogate`

FerroGate, the Token4AI Cloud open-source Rust API Gateway and AI Gateway developed by jamesduan

Subcommands:

- `run` — Run the FerroGate Pingora gateway server (alias: `gateway`)
- `auth` — Run the tenant and RBAC auth REST service
- `control-api` — Run the standalone FerroGate Control Plane API service (issue #359)
- `admin-api` — [DEPRECATED] Alias of `control-api`; run the FerroGate Control Plane API service. Kept for the migration window
- `billing` — Run the token-usage billing REST service
- `storage` — Operate durable storage and migration tooling
- `validate` — Validate configuration and print a summary
- `reload` — Validate a candidate config or reload a running gateway through Admin API
- `hash-key` — Hash a virtual API key secret for durable configuration
- `assets` — Push/pull/list static assets through /v1/assets/* (issue #178)
- `plans` — Create/list/assign sellable subscription plans (issue #168)
- `context` — Manage named Control Plane API client contexts (issue #360): endpoint, default scope, TLS policy, and credential source (never a token value)
- `ops` — Inspect the Control Plane API through the shared typed client (issue #360). The first vertical slice: `ops status`
- `completions` — Generate a shell completion script for the full `ferrogate` command tree (issue #365), including the generic `ctl <group> <verb>` resource families. Writes the script to stdout, e.g. `ferrogate completions bash > /etc/bash_completion.d/ferrogate`
- `ctl` — Control Plane API resource commands (issues #361-#365): every registered resource family, dispatched generically through the shared #360 client.

### `ferrogate run`

Run the FerroGate Pingora gateway server (alias: `gateway`)

Options:

- `-c, --config <CONFIG>` [env: FERROGATE_CONFIG]
- `--upgrade` — Start as the new Pingora process in a graceful upgrade


### `ferrogate auth`

Run the tenant and RBAC auth REST service

Subcommands:

- `serve` — Run the tenant and RBAC REST API service

#### `ferrogate auth serve`

Run the tenant and RBAC REST API service

Options:

- `--listen <LISTEN>` — Address for the auth REST API service [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_LISTEN]
- `--data <DATA>` — Optional YAML data file with tenants, API keys, roles, and bindings [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_DATA]
- `--supabase-dsn <SUPABASE_DSN>` — Supabase PostgreSQL DSN for durable hashed virtual API key resolution [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_SUPABASE_DSN]
- `--supabase-tls-mode <SUPABASE_TLS_MODE>` — Supabase/PostgreSQL TLS mode for durable virtual API key resolution [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_SUPABASE_TLS_MODE]
- `--supabase-tls-ca-cert-path <SUPABASE_TLS_CA_CERT_PATH>` — Optional CA certificate path for Supabase/PostgreSQL TLS validation. Required when `--supabase-tls-mode` is `verify_ca`/`verify_full` against Supabase's pooler, whose chain terminates in the self-signed Supabase Root 2021 CA rather than a system root (#382). Mirrors the gateway's `storage.postgres_tls_ca_cert_path`; unset defers to the system trust store [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_SUPABASE_TLS_CA_CERT_PATH]
- `--supabase-schema <SUPABASE_SCHEMA>` — PostgreSQL schema holding the auth service's own tenant/RBAC tables. Dedicated by default so this service never shares a namespace with the gateway's own `ferrogate_control` schema or the billing service's schema in the same Supabase project (issue #156) [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_SUPABASE_SCHEMA]
- `--supabase-init-schema` — Initialize missing Supabase schema objects before serving [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_SUPABASE_INIT_SCHEMA]
- `--admin-jwt-secret <ADMIN_JWT_SECRET>` — Shared secret used to sign/verify admin-console session tokens (issue #157). Enables the register/login/refresh/logout/me endpoints when set together with --supabase-dsn; prefer --admin-jwt-secret-env to keep the secret out of argv [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_ADMIN_JWT_SECRET]
- `--admin-jwt-secret-env <ADMIN_JWT_SECRET_ENV>` — Environment variable name holding the admin-console JWT secret [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_ADMIN_JWT_SECRET_ENV]
- `--cors-allowed-origin <CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGIN>` — Origin reflected back as Access-Control-Allow-Origin on every response (including OPTIONS preflight), so a separately-deployed admin console frontend (issue #158/#159) can call this service cross-origin. Unset disables CORS headers entirely [env: FERROGATE_AUTH_CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGIN]


### `ferrogate control-api`

Run the standalone FerroGate Control Plane API service (issue #359)

Subcommands:

- `serve` — Run the standalone FerroGate Control Plane API service (issue #359): a dedicated listener that authenticates control-plane callers and proxies the path-compatible /admin/v1/* (+ /v1/assets/*) surface to the gateway configured in the [control_api] config section (the deprecated [admin_api] section is still accepted as an alias)

#### `ferrogate control-api serve`

Run the standalone FerroGate Control Plane API service (issue #359): a dedicated listener that authenticates control-plane callers and proxies the path-compatible /admin/v1/* (+ /v1/assets/*) surface to the gateway configured in the [control_api] config section (the deprecated [admin_api] section is still accepted as an alias)

Options:

- `-c, --config <CONFIG>` [env: FERROGATE_CONFIG]

Subcommands:

- `serve` — [DEPRECATED alias for `control-api serve`] Run the FerroGate Control Plane API service. Retained for the migration window; prints a deprecation notice and behaves identically to `control-api serve`

#### `ferrogate admin-api serve`

[DEPRECATED alias for `control-api serve`] Run the FerroGate Control Plane API service. Retained for the migration window; prints a deprecation notice and behaves identically to `control-api serve`

Options:

- `-c, --config <CONFIG>` [env: FERROGATE_CONFIG]


### `ferrogate billing`

Run the token-usage billing REST service

Subcommands:

- `serve` — Run the token-usage billing REST service

#### `ferrogate billing serve`

Run the token-usage billing REST service

Options:

- `--listen <LISTEN>` — Address for the billing REST API service [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_LISTEN]
- `--pricing <PRICING>` — Optional JSON rate-card file (array of price entries or a full price book object). When omitted, a built-in default rate card is used [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_PRICING]
- `--credits-per-usd <CREDITS_PER_USD>` — Credits charged per settled USD (`credits = total_cost_usd * this`). Overrides the value from the pricing file when set [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_CREDITS_PER_USD]
- `--token <TOKEN>` — Shared secret required on charge/ledger requests (`Authorization: Bearer <token>`). When set, unauthenticated requests are rejected; `/healthz` stays open. Prefer `--token-env` to keep the secret out of argv [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_TOKEN]
- `--token-env <TOKEN_ENV>` — Environment variable name holding the billing shared secret [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_TOKEN_ENV]
- `--supabase-dsn <SUPABASE_DSN>` — Supabase PostgreSQL DSN for durable ledger persistence. When omitted, the ledger is kept in memory only [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_SUPABASE_DSN]
- `--supabase-tls-mode <SUPABASE_TLS_MODE>` — Supabase/PostgreSQL TLS mode for durable ledger persistence [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_SUPABASE_TLS_MODE]
- `--supabase-tls-ca-cert-path <SUPABASE_TLS_CA_CERT_PATH>` — Optional CA certificate path for Supabase/PostgreSQL TLS validation. Required when `--supabase-tls-mode` is `verify_ca`/`verify_full` against Supabase's pooler, whose chain terminates in the self-signed Supabase Root 2021 CA rather than a system root (#382). Mirrors the gateway's `storage.postgres_tls_ca_cert_path`; unset defers to the system trust store [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_SUPABASE_TLS_CA_CERT_PATH]
- `--supabase-schema <SUPABASE_SCHEMA>` — PostgreSQL schema holding the billing service's own ledger/outbox tables. Dedicated by default so this service never shares a namespace with the gateway's own `ferrogate_control` schema or the auth service's schema in the same Supabase project (issue #156) [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_SUPABASE_SCHEMA]
- `--supabase-init-schema` — Initialize missing Supabase schema objects before serving [env: FERROGATE_BILLING_SUPABASE_INIT_SCHEMA]


### `ferrogate storage`

Operate durable storage and migration tooling

Subcommands:

- `migrate-to-supabase` — Migrate legacy durable control-plane state into Supabase

#### `ferrogate storage migrate-to-supabase`

Migrate legacy durable control-plane state into Supabase

Options:

- `--source-provider <SOURCE_PROVIDER>` — Source durable provider. The current migration path supports postgres
- `--source-postgres-dsn <SOURCE_POSTGRES_DSN>` — Source PostgreSQL DSN. Prefer --source-postgres-dsn-env for shell history safety
- `--source-postgres-dsn-env <SOURCE_POSTGRES_DSN_ENV>` — Environment variable containing the source PostgreSQL DSN [env: FERROGATE_MIGRATION_SOURCE_POSTGRES_DSN_ENV]
- `--target-supabase-dsn <TARGET_SUPABASE_DSN>` — Target Supabase DSN. Prefer --target-supabase-dsn-env for shell history safety
- `--target-supabase-dsn-env <TARGET_SUPABASE_DSN_ENV>` — Environment variable containing the target Supabase DSN [env: FERROGATE_MIGRATION_TARGET_SUPABASE_DSN_ENV]
- `--postgres-schema <POSTGRES_SCHEMA>` — PostgreSQL/Supabase schema containing FerroGate storage tables
- `--postgres-tls-mode <POSTGRES_TLS_MODE>` — PostgreSQL/Supabase TLS mode for source and target connections
- `--postgres-tls-ca-cert-path <POSTGRES_TLS_CA_CERT_PATH>` — Optional CA certificate path for PostgreSQL/Supabase TLS validation
- `--dry-run` — Run migration validation and counts without writing the target
- `--execute` — Write the exported source snapshot into Supabase


### `ferrogate validate`

Validate configuration and print a summary

Options:

- `-c, --config <CONFIG>` [env: FERROGATE_CONFIG]


### `ferrogate reload`

Validate a candidate config or reload a running gateway through Admin API

Options:

- `-c, --config <CONFIG>` [env: FERROGATE_CONFIG]
- `--admin-url <ADMIN_URL>` — Running gateway admin API base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_ADMIN_URL]
- `--admin-token <ADMIN_TOKEN>` — Bearer token with admin.write scope for the running gateway admin API [env: FERROGATE_ADMIN_TOKEN]
- `--graceful-upgrade` — Start a new `ferrogate run --upgrade` process and send SIGQUIT to the active pid


### `ferrogate hash-key`

Hash a virtual API key secret for durable configuration

Options:

- `--secret <SECRET>` [env: FERROGATE_KEY_SECRET]


### `ferrogate assets`

Push/pull/list static assets through /v1/assets/* (issue #178)

Subcommands:

- `push` — Upload a local file as a new asset version
- `pull` — Download an asset's content to a local file (or stdout with `-`)
- `list` — List a tenant's assets, optionally filtered by asset type
- `delete` — Delete one asset version

#### `ferrogate assets push`

Upload a local file as a new asset version

Arguments:

- `<PATH>` — Local file to upload

Options:

- `--gateway-url <GATEWAY_URL>` — Running gateway base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_GATEWAY_URL]
- `--api-key <API_KEY>` — Virtual API key with assets.read/assets.write scope [env: FERROGATE_API_KEY]
- `--type <ASSET_TYPE>` — Asset type, e.g. cli_tool, mcp_manifest, skill_bundle, static_site, config_file
- `--name <NAME>` — Asset name
- `--content-type <CONTENT_TYPE>` — Content-Type header to record; guessed from the file extension when omitted
- `--platform <PLATFORM>` — Platform/arch variant (target triple), e.g. linux-x86_64 (#260). One logical version can carry several per-platform artifacts
- `--channel <CHANNEL>` — Also point this channel (latest/stable/canary or a free-form tag) at the pushed version (#260)


#### `ferrogate assets pull`

Download an asset's content to a local file (or stdout with `-`)

Options:

- `--gateway-url <GATEWAY_URL>` — Running gateway base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_GATEWAY_URL]
- `--api-key <API_KEY>` — Virtual API key with assets.read/assets.write scope [env: FERROGATE_API_KEY]
- `--type <ASSET_TYPE>` — Asset type
- `--name <NAME>` — Asset name
- `--platform <PLATFORM>` — Platform/arch variant (target triple) to select on pull (#260)
- `--output <OUTPUT>` — Output file for `pull` (defaults to stdout when omitted or `-`)


#### `ferrogate assets list`

List a tenant's assets, optionally filtered by asset type

Options:

- `--gateway-url <GATEWAY_URL>` — Running gateway base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_GATEWAY_URL]
- `--api-key <API_KEY>` — Virtual API key with assets.read/assets.write scope [env: FERROGATE_API_KEY]
- `--type <ASSET_TYPE>` — Restrict the list to one asset type


#### `ferrogate assets delete`

Delete one asset version

Options:

- `--gateway-url <GATEWAY_URL>` — Running gateway base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_GATEWAY_URL]
- `--api-key <API_KEY>` — Virtual API key with assets.read/assets.write scope [env: FERROGATE_API_KEY]
- `--type <ASSET_TYPE>` — Asset type
- `--name <NAME>` — Asset name
- `--platform <PLATFORM>` — Platform/arch variant (target triple) to select on pull (#260)
- `--output <OUTPUT>` — Output file for `pull` (defaults to stdout when omitted or `-`)


### `ferrogate plans`

Create/list/assign sellable subscription plans (issue #168)

Subcommands:

- `create` — Create a new sellable plan
- `list` — List every plan
- `assign` — Assign an existing plan to an existing tenant

#### `ferrogate plans create`

Create a new sellable plan

Options:

- `--gateway-url <GATEWAY_URL>` — Running gateway base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_GATEWAY_URL]
- `--api-key <API_KEY>` — Virtual API key with admin.read/admin.write scope [env: FERROGATE_API_KEY]
- `--id <ID>` — Plan id; defaults to the slug when omitted
- `--name <NAME>` — Human-readable plan name
- `--slug <SLUG>` — URL-safe plan identifier
- `--mcp-enabled` — Grant MCP tool governance access
- `--self-hosted-workers-enabled` — Grant self-hosted/managed worker dispatch eligibility
- `--asset-hosting-enabled` — Grant /v1/assets/* hosting access
- `--extension-tools-enabled` — Grant Extension-backend /v1/tools/execute access
- `--default-rpm-limit <DEFAULT_RPM_LIMIT>` — Default requests-per-minute cap for tenants on this plan
- `--default-tpm-limit <DEFAULT_TPM_LIMIT>` — Default tokens-per-minute cap for tenants on this plan
- `--default-monthly-budget-usd <DEFAULT_MONTHLY_BUDGET_USD>` — Default monthly budget (USD) for tenants on this plan


#### `ferrogate plans list`

List every plan

Options:

- `--gateway-url <GATEWAY_URL>` — Running gateway base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_GATEWAY_URL]
- `--api-key <API_KEY>` — Virtual API key with admin.read/admin.write scope [env: FERROGATE_API_KEY]


#### `ferrogate plans assign`

Assign an existing plan to an existing tenant

Options:

- `--gateway-url <GATEWAY_URL>` — Running gateway base URL, for example http://127.0.0.1:8080 [env: FERROGATE_GATEWAY_URL]
- `--api-key <API_KEY>` — Virtual API key with admin.read/admin.write scope [env: FERROGATE_API_KEY]
- `--tenant-id <TENANT_ID>` — Tenant account id to update
- `--plan-id <PLAN_ID>` — Plan id to assign to the tenant


### `ferrogate context`

Manage named Control Plane API client contexts (issue #360): endpoint, default scope, TLS policy, and credential source (never a token value)

Subcommands:

- `create` — Create (or, with --overwrite, replace) a named context
- `list` — List defined contexts; the current one is marked with `*`
- `show` — Show one context's non-secret settings
- `use` — Select the default context used when no --context/env override is given
- `delete` — Delete a named context

#### `ferrogate context create`

Create (or, with --overwrite, replace) a named context

Arguments:

- `<NAME>` — Context name (e.g. production)

Options:

- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Control Plane API base URL, for example https://control.example.com
- `--tenant <TENANT>` — Default tenant applied to commands that accept one
- `--project <PROJECT>` — Default project
- `--workspace <WORKSPACE>` — Default workspace
- `--token-env <VAR>` — Name of the environment variable holding the bearer token, read at call time. The token value is never stored in the context
- `--token-stdin` — Read the bearer token from stdin at call time instead of an env var
- `--ca-bundle <PATH>` — Path to a PEM CA bundle used to verify this endpoint's TLS certificate in addition to the system trust roots (for a private/internal CA)
- `--insecure-skip-tls-verify` — Skip TLS certificate verification for this context (opt-in, insecure; intended for local self-signed dev endpoints only)
- `--use` — After creating, select this context as the current one
- `--overwrite` — Overwrite an existing context with the same name


#### `ferrogate context list`

List defined contexts; the current one is marked with `*`


#### `ferrogate context show`

Show one context's non-secret settings

Arguments:

- `<NAME>` — Context name


#### `ferrogate context use`

Select the default context used when no --context/env override is given

Arguments:

- `<NAME>` — Context name


#### `ferrogate context delete`

Delete a named context

Arguments:

- `<NAME>` — Context name


### `ferrogate ops`

Inspect the Control Plane API through the shared typed client (issue #360). The first vertical slice: `ops status`

Options:

- `--context <CONTEXT>` — Use a specific named context instead of the current one
- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Override the Control Plane API endpoint URL
- `--tenant <TENANT>` — Override the tenant for this invocation
- `--token-env <VAR>` — Read the bearer token from this environment variable
- `--token-stdin` — Read the bearer token from stdin
- `--timeout-millis <MILLIS>` — Per-request timeout in milliseconds
- `--output <FORMAT>` — Output format: table or json
- `--non-interactive` — Do not prompt; fail instead of asking (for scripts/CI)

Subcommands:

- `status` — Show Control Plane API system status (GET /admin/v1/status) through the shared typed client

#### `ferrogate ops status`

Show Control Plane API system status (GET /admin/v1/status) through the shared typed client


### `ferrogate completions`

Generate a shell completion script for the full `ferrogate` command tree (issue #365), including the generic `ctl <group> <verb>` resource families. Writes the script to stdout, e.g. `ferrogate completions bash > /etc/bash_completion.d/ferrogate`

Arguments:

- `<SHELL>` — Target shell (bash, zsh, fish, powershell, elvish)

Subcommands:

- `tenant-accounts` — Manage tenant accounts
- `tenants` — List gateway tenants (admin view)
- `projects` — Manage projects
- `workspaces` — Manage workspaces
- `plans` — Manage plans
- `quota-policies` — Manage quota policies

- `api-keys` — Manage gateway admin API keys
- `roles` — Manage RBAC roles
- `permissions` — Manage RBAC permissions
- `access-policies` — Manage named access policies
- `tenant-roles` — Manage tenant role bindings
- `agent-workflows` — Manage agent workflow definitions
- `agent-schedules` — Manage agent run schedules and their fire history
- `agent-upstreams` — Manage agent upstream bindings
- `agent-runs` — Start and inspect agent runs
- `agent-jobs` — Submit, observe, collect, and cancel long-running agent jobs
- `self-hosted-workers` — Manage self-hosted workers and their lifecycle
- `managed-workers` — List managed worker pools
- `managed-worker-sessions` — List managed worker sessions
- `self-hosted-worker-records` — List immutable self-hosted worker records
- `self-hosted-runs` — Inspect self-hosted run timelines
- `mcp-servers` — Manage registered MCP servers
- `mcp-identity` — Manage per-server MCP identity grants
- `tool-sessions` — Inspect tool session event streams
- `tools` — Inspect the admin tool catalog
- `tool-approvals` — Review and resolve pending tool-call approvals
- `guardrail-policies` — Manage guardrail policies and their immutable revisions
- `guardrail-evaluations` — Inspect the guardrail evaluation event stream
- `investigations` — Join cross-plane evidence for one request, trace, or agent run
- `assets` — Manage the asset registry, versions, and lifecycle
- `asset-transfer` — Presigned asset upload and download flows
- `asset-channels` — Manage asset release channels and promotion
- `site-domains` — Manage static-site custom domain bindings
- `prompt-templates` — Manage the prompt-template catalog
- `skill-packages` — Manage the skill-package catalog
- `plugins` — Manage the plugin catalog
- `catalog` — Inspect read-only model/provider/extension catalogs
- `dashboard` — Read the admin dashboard aggregate summary
- `wallets` — Manage tenant credit wallets and settlement
- `payment-methods` — Manage stored payment methods
- `billing-events` — Inspect billing events and outbox dead letters
- `usage` — Inspect usage aggregates, reports, metering, and agent cost-burn

- `audit-events` — List retained Admin API audit events
- `observed-agent-activity` — List observed agent/run activity correlations
- `system` — Inspect system status, readiness, and observability
- `provider-health` — List provider health status
- `config` — Validate and reload gateway configuration

- `gateway-configs` — Manage gateway config profiles

#### `ferrogate ctl tenant-accounts`

Manage tenant accounts

Subcommands:

- `list` — List tenant accounts
- `get` — Show a tenant account
- `create` — Create a tenant account
- `replace` — Replace a tenant account
- `update` — Update a tenant account
- `resolved-defaults` — Show a tenant's resolved defaults
- `assign-plan` — Assign a subscription plan to a tenant

##### `ferrogate ctl tenant-accounts list`

List tenant accounts

Arguments:

- `<SEGMENT>...` — Resource id path segment(s) — e.g. a single id, or a `scope_type` `scope_id` pair for composite keys. Omitted for collection verbs (`list`/`create`)

Options:

- `--data <JSON>` — Inline JSON request document for a write verb
- `--file <PATH>` — Path to a JSON request document for a write verb (`-` reads stdin)
- `--limit <N>` — Page size for a list verb
- `--offset <N>` — Starting offset for a list verb
- `--filter <KEY=VALUE>` — Server-side list filter as `KEY=VALUE` (repeatable)
- `--sort <FIELD>` — Server-side sort key for a list verb; prefix with `-` for descending. Repeatable and order-preserving, so the first key is the primary sort. NOT HONORED BY THE SERVER TODAY: no Control Plane API operation declares a `sort` query parameter, so the key is forwarded verbatim and currently ignored; a note is printed to stderr on every use
- `--all-pages` — Fetch every page of a list verb instead of a single server page, using `--limit` as the page size. Mutually exclusive with `--offset`, which selects one page
- `--context <CONTEXT>` — Use a specific named context instead of the current one
- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Override the Control Plane API endpoint URL
- `--tenant <TENANT>` — Override the tenant for this invocation
- `--token-env <VAR>` — Read the bearer token from this environment variable
- `--token-stdin` — Read the bearer token from stdin
- `--timeout-millis <MILLIS>` — Per-request timeout in milliseconds
- `--output <FORMAT>` — Output format: table or json
- `--non-interactive` — Do not prompt; fail instead of asking (for scripts/CI)


##### `ferrogate ctl tenant-accounts get`

Show a tenant account

Arguments:

- `<SEGMENT>...` — Resource id path segment(s) — e.g. a single id, or a `scope_type` `scope_id` pair for composite keys. Omitted for collection verbs (`list`/`create`)

Options:

- `--data <JSON>` — Inline JSON request document for a write verb
- `--file <PATH>` — Path to a JSON request document for a write verb (`-` reads stdin)
- `--limit <N>` — Page size for a list verb
- `--offset <N>` — Starting offset for a list verb
- `--filter <KEY=VALUE>` — Server-side list filter as `KEY=VALUE` (repeatable)
- `--sort <FIELD>` — Server-side sort key for a list verb; prefix with `-` for descending. Repeatable and order-preserving, so the first key is the primary sort. NOT HONORED BY THE SERVER TODAY: no Control Plane API operation declares a `sort` query parameter, so the key is forwarded verbatim and currently ignored; a note is printed to stderr on every use
- `--all-pages` — Fetch every page of a list verb instead of a single server page, using `--limit` as the page size. Mutually exclusive with `--offset`, which selects one page
- `--context <CONTEXT>` — Use a specific named context instead of the current one
- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Override the Control Plane API endpoint URL
- `--tenant <TENANT>` — Override the tenant for this invocation
- `--token-env <VAR>` — Read the bearer token from this environment variable

- `--timeout-millis <MILLIS>` — Per-request timeout in milliseconds
- `--output <FORMAT>` — Output format: table or json
- `--non-interactive` — Do not prompt; fail instead of asking (for scripts/CI)


##### `ferrogate ctl tenant-accounts create`

Create a tenant account

Arguments:

- `--limit <N>` — Page size for a list verb
- `--offset <N>` — Starting offset for a list verb
- `--filter <KEY=VALUE>` — Server-side list filter as `KEY=VALUE` (repeatable)
- `--sort <FIELD>` — Server-side sort key for a list verb; prefix with `-` for descending. Repeatable and order-preserving, so the first key is the primary sort. - `--context <CONTEXT>` — Use a specific named context instead of the current one
- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Override the Control Plane API endpoint URL

- `--token-env <VAR>` — Read the bearer token from this environment variable

- `<SEGMENT>...` — Resource id path segment(s) — e.g. a single id, or a `scope_type` `scope_id` pair for composite keys. Omitted for collection verbs (`list`/`create`)

Options:

- `--data <JSON>` — Inline JSON request document for a write verb
- `--file <PATH>` — Path to a JSON request document for a write verb (`-` reads stdin)
- `--limit <N>` — Page size for a list verb
- `--offset <N>` — Starting offset for a list verb
- `--filter <KEY=VALUE>` — Server-side list filter as `KEY=VALUE` (repeatable)
- `--sort <FIELD>` — Server-side sort key for a list verb; prefix with `-` for descending. Repeatable and order-preserving, so the first key is the primary sort. NOT HONORED BY THE SERVER TODAY: no Control Plane API operation declares a `sort` query parameter, so the key is forwarded verbatim and currently ignored; a note is printed to stderr on every use
- `--all-pages` — Fetch every page of a list verb instead of a single server page, using `--limit` as the page size. Mutually exclusive with `--offset`, which selects one page
- `--context <CONTEXT>` — Use a specific named context instead of the current one
- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Override the Control Plane API endpoint URL
- `--tenant <TENANT>` — Override the tenant for this invocation
- `--token-env <VAR>` — Read the bearer token from this environment variable
- `--token-stdin` — Read the bearer token from stdin
- `--timeout-millis <MILLIS>` — Per-request timeout in milliseconds
- `--output <FORMAT>` — Output format: table or json
- `--non-interactive` — Do not prompt; fail instead of asking (for scripts/CI)

- `<SEGMENT>...` — Resource id path segment(s) — e.g. a single id, or a `scope_type` `scope_id` pair for composite keys. Omitted for collection verbs (`list`/`create`)

Options:

- `--data <JSON>` — Inline JSON request document for a write verb
- `--file <PATH>` — Path to a JSON request document for a write verb (`-` reads stdin)
- `--limit <N>` — Page size for a list verb
- `--offset <N>` — Starting offset for a list verb
- `--filter <KEY=VALUE>` — Server-side list filter as `KEY=VALUE` (repeatable)
- `--sort <FIELD>` — Server-side sort key for a list verb; prefix with `-` for descending. Repeatable and order-preserving, so the first key is the primary sort. NOT HONORED BY THE SERVER TODAY: no Control Plane API operation declares a `sort` query parameter, so the key is forwarded verbatim and currently ignored; a note is printed to stderr on every use
- `--all-pages` — Fetch every page of a list verb instead of a single server page, using `--limit` as the page size. Mutually exclusive with `--offset`, which selects one page
- `--context <CONTEXT>` — Use a specific named context instead of the current one
- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Override the Control Plane API endpoint URL
- `--tenant <TENANT>` — Override the tenant for this invocation
- `--token-env <VAR>` — Read the bearer token from this environment variable
- `--token-stdin` — Read the bearer token from stdin
- `--timeout-millis <MILLIS>` — Per-request timeout in milliseconds
- `--output <FORMAT>` — Output format: table or json
- `--non-interactive` — Do not prompt; fail instead of asking (for scripts/CI)

- `<SEGMENT>...` — Resource id path segment(s) — e.g. a single id, or a `scope_type` `scope_id` pair for composite keys. Omitted for collection verbs (`list`/`create`)

Options:

- `--data <JSON>` — Inline JSON request document for a write verb
- `--file <PATH>` — Path to a JSON request document for a write verb (`-` reads stdin)
- `--limit <N>` — Page size for a list verb
- `--offset <N>` — Starting offset for a list verb
- `--filter <KEY=VALUE>` — Server-side list filter as `KEY=VALUE` (repeatable)
- `--sort <FIELD>` — Server-side sort key for a list verb; prefix with `-` for descending. Repeatable and order-preserving, so the first key is the primary sort. NOT HONORED BY THE SERVER TODAY: no Control Plane API operation declares a `sort` query parameter, so the key is forwarded verbatim and currently ignored; a note is printed to stderr on every use
- `--all-pages` — Fetch every page of a list verb instead of a single server page, using `--limit` as the page size. Mutually exclusive with `--offset`, which selects one page
- `--context <CONTEXT>` — Use a specific named context instead of the current one
- `--endpoint <ENDPOINT>` — Override the Control Plane API endpoint URL
- `--tenant <TENANT>` — Override the tenant for this invocation
- `--token-env <VAR>` — Read the bearer token from this environment variable
- `--token-stdin` — Read the bearer token from stdin
- `--timeout-millis <MILLIS>` — Per-request timeout in milliseconds
- `--output <FORMAT>` — Output format: table or json
- `--non-interactive` — Do not prompt; fail instead of asking (for scripts/CI)
